K

~

S

_∞^L +
t
_∥
)

}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\prod_{k=1}^{\ell}\left\lceil\frac{2\rho\sqrt{K}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{k}\rVert_{2}}\right\rceil\sum_{\alpha_{\ell+1}\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathds{1}\Biggl\{\alpha_{\ell+1}\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\alpha_{\ell^{\prime}}\frac{\mathbf{f}_{\ell^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}-\frac{\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{t}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell+1}

∈[-1,1]

×

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\mathds{1}\Biggl\{\alpha_{\ell+1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\frac{\mathbf{f}_{\ell+1}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\alpha_{\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{\ell^{\prime\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\frac{\mathbf{f}_{\ell^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}-\sum_{\ell^{\prime\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\frac{\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{t}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(c)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\prod_{k=1}^{\ell+1}\left\lceil\frac{2\rho\sqrt{K}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{k}\rVert_{2}}\right\rceil\mathds{1}\Biggl\{\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L}\alpha_{\ell^{\prime}}\sum_{\ell^{\prime\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\frac{\mathbf{f}_{\ell^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}-\sum_{\ell^{\prime\prime}=\ell+2}^{L}\frac{\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}^{\mathsf{T}}\mathbf{t}}{\lVert\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{2}^{2}}\mathbf{g}_{\ell^{\prime\prime}}

∈∑_ℓ”=ℓ+2^L [-1,1]
